eṉṉum puṟattuṟai, koṇṭu kūṟap peṟṟatu ituvākum.
kaṭainilai - viṉāvi niṟṟal eṉap poruḷ kāṇalum uṇṭu.
periyōr cēṇiṭai varutalāṟ piṟanta
varuttam tīra vāyilkāk kiṉṟōr
eṉvara viṉaiyiṟaik kiyamputi nīyeṉak
kaṭaikkaṇiṉ ṟuraippatu kaṭainilai yākum	-muttuvī. 1099
paricil nīṭṭikkap paruvaraluṟṟa pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ pala puṟanāṉūṟṟil iṭam peṟṟuḷa. kaṭainilaikku muṉṉōṭi avai eṉka. (127,382,383,384,391,392,393,394,395,398)
kaṭainteṭuttal:
kaṭainteṭuttal = akavaiyai mīṟiya aṟivu.
tayiraik kaṭaintu veṇṇey eṭuttaliliruntu vanta vaḻakkuccol itu. pāliṉ aḷavu kāya vaikkum poḻutu cuṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ piraiyiṭṭut tayirākkik kaṭaintāl veṇṇey tiraḷum, attiraḷ ciṟitāyiṉum appāliṉ ūṭṭam aṉaittum attiraḷil aṭaṅki viṭukiṟatu. atupōl ciṟiya akavaiyil periya āḷukkuriya aṟivu, āṟṟal,ūṭṭam aṉaittum attiraḷil aṭaṅki viṭukiṟatu.atupōl ciṟiya viṉāvutal iruppiṉ kaṭain teṭuttavaṉ avaṉ eṉpar. āṉāl, pārāṭṭutalāka aḵtu amaiyāmal ikaḻtalāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉil akavaikku viñciyatum poruntātatum ākiya aṟivuk kūrppē veḷippāṭāka iruttalāl.
ciṟantatāka iruntāl ciṟupperumai eṉṟum, ciṟumutuk kuṟaivu eṉṟum collappaṭum.
kaṭaimaṭai:
ēri, kuḷam, kālvāy nīr, viḷai nilaṅkaḷil pāyntu, kaṭaiciyāka veḷiyēṟum maṭai, kaṭaimaṭai yākum. viḷai nilaṅkaḷukku nīr mutaṟkaṇ varum maṭai talaimaṭai.
talaimaṭaiyālē nīrpāyum kaṭaimaṭaiyālē viḻavum        								(te.i.kal.to.23:490)
kaṭaiyam:
makaḷir kālaṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟu kaṭaiyam. kaṭaical vēlai mikkatu ātalāl peṟṟa peyar. kaṭakam kaivaḷai, tōḷvaḷai āyavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum āṭavaraṇi.
kaṭakakkai puṭaittu nakkāṉ 	- eṉpār kampar(ayōt. 413)
kaṭkam:
kaṭkam:1
kamukkam > kakkam > kaṭkam = maṟaiviṭam; tōḷpaṭṭaiyiṉ uṭpakuti.
kaṭkam:2
tōṭpaṭṭaiyiṉ uṭpakutiyil vaikkum vāḷ.
kaṭci:
kaṭṭappaṭṭatu; kūṭic cērntu palavaṟṟāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu kaṭci - kūṭu eṉṉum poruḷatu. paṟavai muṭṭai iṭuvataṟkāka āṇ paṟavaiyum peṇpaṟavaiyum kūṭik kaṭṭiyatu kaṭci eṉappaṭṭatu.
īṉil eṉṟa atu. uṟaiviṭamum āyiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ vilaṅkukaḷ uṟaiyum kukaiyāy āyatu. innāḷil kaṭci eṉpatu araciyal amaippup peyarākiyatu. araciyalil āḷum. kaṭci, etirkkaṭci eṉa amaintu karuttu mōtal ēṟpaṭuvatāl kaṭci kaṭṭutal eṉpatu kūṭi etirttal poruḷ tantatu. viḷaiyāṭṭil kaṭci pirittal vaḻakkam.
eritta kaṭci, eriyāk kaṭci eṉac camayap pōrk kaṭcikaḷ mēṭai yēṟṟap paṭavum āyatu! kaṭci, kaṭci māṟi eṉa virivākkiviṭṭatu makkaḷ tērtal āṭci.
kaṭṭakkāl;
kuṭṭaik kāl eṉpatu kaṭṭaikkāl ākik kaṭṭakkāl eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatu itu. kuṟuṅkālai yuṭaiya paṉṟiyaik kaṭṭakkāl eṉpatu tiruvillipputtūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. kaṭṭappiḷḷai neṭṭappiḷḷai eṉpatu nāṭṭuppuṟap pāṭṭu.
kaṭṭaṭam - kaṭṭiṭam:
kaṭṭu = aṭam = kaṭṭaṭam. itu kaṭṭu eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyaiyum, aṭam eṉṉum iṟuti nilaiyaiyum koṇṭatu. kaṭṭaṭam eṉpatu kaṭṭum toḻil vaḻiyāka vanta peyar. kaṭṭutal eṉpatu kaṟkaṭṭu, ceṅkaṟkaṭṭu, kaṭṭaṭa vēlai muṭintatu; pūccut tāṉ naṭakka vēṇṭum! vīṭṭaik kaṭṭippār; vīṭu kaṭṭi viḷaiyāṭal ivai ellām kaṭṭaṭac collai viḷakkuvaṉa.
kaṭṭu = iṭam = kaṭṭiṭam. kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭutaṟkeṉat tērntu koṇṭa iṭam - maṉaiyiṭam - kaṭṭiṭam. iṭam vāṅki viṭṭēṉ eṉpatu kaṭṭa iṭam vāṅki viṭṭēṉ eṉṉum poruḷatu. avviṭattil kaṭṭaṭam iṉimēltāṉ kaṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭatu.
kaṭṭiṭam, iṭappeyar; kaṭṭaṭam toḻiṟ peyar. muṉṉatil iṭam iṭappeyar; piṉṉatil aṭam toḻiṟpeyar.
kaṭṭaṭa vēlai ceyyum kalviyaṟiyār eppaṭi eḻutiṉālum, poṟiyiyal kaṟṟa aṟiñarum iraṇṭum oṉṟāka eḻutalāmā? oṉṟeṉa eṇṇalāmā? oppanta viḷamparaṅkaḷ, ceytit tāḷkaḷ ivaṟṟilellām ippiḻai eṉṟāl, aṟiñarkaḷum ippiḻaiyai viṭṭārkaḷā? iraṇṭum caritāṉ eṉṟu ettaṉai coṟkaḷaic colvārkaḷō! ettaṉai kālam colvārkaḷō!
kaṭṭaṇam:
kaṭṭaṇam:1
kaṭṭu + aṇ + am = kaṭṭaṇam = kaṭṭa vēṇṭiya tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa tokai. kaṭṭāyam pōlvatu añcaṟ kaṭṭaṇam, miṉkaṭṭaṇam, carakkuk kaṭṭaṇam, kalvik kaṭṭaṇam eṉpavai vaḻakkil uḷḷavai.
kaṭṭaṇam:2
kaṭṭaṇam eṉpatu pāṭai eṉṉum poruḷil nākarkōyil vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. pāṭu eṉpatu paṭutal; kaṇṇimai mūṭutal; uṟaṅkutal; cettārai eṭuttuc cellum paṭukkai amaippu. kaṇpāṭu = uṟakkam pāṭivīṭu = paṭukkai iṭam. kaṭṭaikaḷai vaittuk kaṭṭutal vaḻi vanta peyar itu.
kaṭṭam:
kaṭṭam:1
kaṭṭu + am = kaṭṭam.
kaṭṭuppāṭāka viḷaiyāṭutaṟku aḷavu kōlākap pōṭappaṭṭatu kaṭṭam. enta viḷaiyāṭṭuk kaḷamum aḷavīṭu amaintatu aṟika. kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭutaṟkum kaṭṭumāṉam ceytaṟkum oṉṟai otta aḷavil ceytaṟkum kaṭṭu vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu. tāyakkaṭṭam, cūtu kaṭṭam, kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭumāṉam, kaṭṭaḷaik kal, aṟak kaṭṭaḷai eṉpaṉavellām kaṭṭu vaḻiyē amaintavai. kaṭṭu = varampu.
kaṭṭu vaḻiyē kaṭṭurai, kaṭṭāyam, kaṭṭāri eṉpaṉavum āyiṉa.
	kaṭṭu + urai		=	kaṭṭurai; kaṭṭamainta urai.
	kaṭṭu + āyam		= 	kaṭṭāyam; aḷaviṭṭa vari.
	kaṭṭu + āri		=	kaṭṭāri; aḷavāka veṭṭum karuvi.
	kaṭṭu + ārppu	= 	kaṭṭārppu > kaṭṭāppu. aḷaviṭṭuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa	 tōppu. kāṭu, nilam  mutaliyaṉa.
kaṭṭam:2
kaṭṭip pōṭṭa oṉṟaṉ iyakkam, illāmal oḻiyum. nōyt tuyar piṇi eṉappaṭutalai eṇṇalām. piṇiyai oppatu kaṭṭu eṉpatu. kaṭṭip pōṭappaṭṭavar aṭaiyum tuyar karutik kūṟap paṭṭa col kaṭṭam ākum. ataṉaik kaṣṭam ākkiyatu moḻikkēṭām.
x.neh.:
iṭṭam > iṣṭam. iṭuvatai virumpuvatu iṭṭam; viruppam.
kaṭṭaḷai:
ceṟittu aḷavāka amaikkappaṭṭatu; aḷaviṭṭuk kāṇa amaintatu varampiṭṭu amaitta amaippu; āṇai.
ceṅkal aṟuppataṟku amainta kaṭṭaḷaic caṭṭam, ataṉuḷ ceṟikkum maṇceṟippu, piṟa toḻilakak kalloṭum otta aḷavīṭu eṉpavai karutuka. poṉmāṟṟu aṟiya amainta kal, kaṭṭaḷaik kallām.
perumaikkum ēṉaic ciṟumaikkum tattam
karumamē kaṭṭaḷaik kal	-tiruk. 505
eṉpatu poṉmāṟṟaṟiyum kallām. iṉṉārāl ēṟpaṭuttap paṭum aṟam eṉpataṟkām aṟakkaṭṭaḷai innāḷil perukkamām. aṟavōr kaṭṭaḷai, ācāṉ kaṭṭaḷai eṉpavai āṇai eṉṉum poruḷatām.
kaṭṭaḷaik kalippā:
caṅkac kāṉṟōrkku muṟpaṭṭōr kaṭṭaḷai aṭiyāl ceyyuḷ ceytalai vaḻakkamākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. tolkāppiyar kūṟum aṭikaḷukkuriya eḻuttu pōla amaintatu kaṭṭaḷai eṉṉum collāṭci.
kaṭṭaḷaik kalittuṟai eṉpatu nēracaiyil toṭaṅkum aṭikkup patiṉāṟu eḻuttaiyum niraiyacaiyil toṭaṅkum aṭikkup patiṉēḻu   eḻuttaiyum koṇṭirukkum. kaṭṭaḷaik kalippā ōraṭiyai iraṇṭu araiyākavum araiyaṭiyai nāṟcīr aḷaviṉatākavum, avvaraiyaṭi nēril toṭaṅkiṉ patiṉōreḻuttum, niraiyil toṭaṅkiṉ paṉṉīreḻuttum peṟṟu varum. yāppāl peṟṟa peyar nūlukku āyiṟṟu.
kaṭṭaḷaik kalippā nāṭṭuṅ kālai
orumāk kūviḷam oru mūṉṟiyaiya
nērpati ṉoṉṟu niraipaṉṉī reḻuttāy
naṭantaṭip pātiyāy nāṉkaṭi ottavāy
varuvatiṉṟu vaḻaṅkum neṟiyē	-toṉ. vi. 236
kaṭṭāṭi:
kaṭṭi eṭuttuc ceṉṟu tuṇikaḷai aṭittu veḷukkum calavaiyaraik kaṭṭāṭi eṉpatu yāḻppāṇa vaḻakku. kaṭṭu eṉpatu poti; potiyait tōḷil mutukil koṇṭu celvatum, kaḻutaimēl ēṟṟic celvatum iṉṟum aritākak kāṇum kāṭcikaḷām.
niraipparap poṟaiya naraippuṟak kaḻutai	-akam. 207
eṉpatu caṅkat toṭar.
kaṭṭāntarai:
kaṭṭu + ām + tarai = kaṭṭāntarai.
keṭṭimikka - vaṉmaiyāṉa - nilam kaṭṭāntarai eṉpatām. uḻavukku ākāta nilam. kaṭṭutal = veṭṭutal. veṭṭip pakkuvap paṭutti viḷainila mākkat takka iṭam.
x.neh.:
kollai, ceṟu.
kaṭṭāppu:
kaṭṭu ārppu > kaṭṭāppu = nerukkamākak kaṭṭutal.
kōṭṭam, tōppu, payirnilam ākiyavaṟṟil āṭu māṭu pukāmal iṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭum vēli, allatu nerukkamāka amainta uyir vēli kaṭṭāppu eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭutal kaṇṭamaṉūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. potuvil teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakkamākum.
kaṭṭāyam:
kaṭṭu + āyam = kaṭṭāyam = vari celuttal pōṟ kaṇṭippu.
kaṭṭu = celuttu; āyam = vari. tavirkka muṭiyāmal ceytē āka vēṇṭiyatu kaṭṭāyamākum. ataṉaic ceyyat tavaṟiṉāl taṇṭaṉaiyil iruntu tappa muṭiyā nilaimaiyatu.
kaṭṭāṉ:
narampu nār ākiyavaṟṟāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu uṭal. ātalāl yākkai(ākkai) eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. yāttal, yāppu eṉpavai kaṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷatu. kaṭṭāṉa uṭal eṉpatai viḷaṅkac ceyvatu elumpu ākum. elumpaik kaṭṭāṉ eṉpatu maturai vaḻakku.
kaṭṭi:
kaṭṭi:1
maṇtiraṇṭu ceṟintatu. pakkuvam vāyā uḻavil nilam kaṭṭi kaṭṭiyākap peyarum kaṭṭi, tiraṇṭu ceṟintatu; valiyatu. maṇkaṭṭi, veḷḷikkaṭṭi, taṅkakkaṭṭi, paṉikkaṭṭi eṉa āyatu.
kaṭṭi:2
kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṭam kaṭṭi āyatu. kalluk kaṭṭi
kaṭṭi:3
kaṭṭi eṉpāṉ valiya vīraṉ. kaṅkaṉ kaṭṭi
kaṭṭi:4
uṭalil uṇṭākum tacaittiraṭci kaṭṭikku maruntu kāvimaṇ. ataṟku maṇkaṭṭi > makkaṭṭi eṉpatu ma.va.
kaṭṭi:5
pāku, carukkaraik kaṭṭi. kaṭṭi pūcik kaṭuttīṟṟal uraiyāciriyarkaḷ. citta maruttuva iyal cārntatu.
kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟu:
kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟu = kaṟṟuk koṭutta kalvi.
kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟṟiṉ aḷavu mikumā? cuvaitāṉ mikumā? tanta aḷavē aḷavāy amaiyum. atu pōl kaṟṟuk koṭutta aḷavilēyē amaiyum kalvi, kaṭṭik koṭut cōṟākac collappaṭum kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟum kaṟṟuk koṭutta kalviyum enta maṭṭō anta maṭṭō eṉṉum paḻamoḻi. ivvaḻakkut toṭariṉ poruḷai viḷakkum. kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟu, kaṭṭuc cōṟu eṉappaṭum. tōḷil ataṉaip pōṭṭuk koṇṭu pōṉa vaḻakkattāl tōṭkōppu eṉpatum atu.
kaṭṭik koḷḷal:
kaṭṭik koḷḷal = tirumaṇam ceytal.
tirumaṇam ceytalait tāli kaṭṭu eṉpatu vaḻakkam. tirumaṇa nikaḻvil kaṭṭāyam iṭam peṟuvatu tālikaṭṭu. tālikaṭṭu muṭintuviṭṭāl tirumaṇa viḻā muṭintatu eṉap pantiyil uṭkārum vaḻakkamē ataṉait terivikkum. vāḻttiṉāl eṉṉa? vāḻttu tirumaṇam āvatillai! tāli kaṭṭutalē tirumaṇamākak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. maṟṟai maṟṟaic caṭaṅkukaḷum kūṭa mutaṉmaiyillai. ataṉāltāṉ, tirumaṇa viḻāvil tāli kaṭṭa maṟantatu pōla eṉṉum paḻamoḻi eḻuntatu. tāli kaṭṭa maṟantāl tirumaṇamē naṭantatākātu eṉpatu teḷivu. ātalāl tāli kāṭṭal illāmalum, tāli kaṭṭal uṇṭu eṉpatē poruḷām. muṭiccup pōṭutal kaṭṭutaltāṉē. mālai māṟṟik koḷḷal, viralāḻi māṟṟik koḷḷal, pativu ceytal eṉpavai putiya varavukaḷ.
kaṭṭippōṭutal:
kaṭṭip pōṭutal = aṭaṅkac ceytal.
kayiṟṟāl kaṭṭutaltāṉ kaṭṭutal eṉpatillai. collāl kaṭṭutalum kaṭṭē. kaṭṭaḷai, kaṭṭurai, kaṭṭuppāṭu, kaṭṭuppaṭuttal, kaṭṭumāṉam eṉpaṉavellām kayiṟṟoṭu toṭarpillāk kaṭṭukaḷē. cilar kotittu eḻum nilaiyilum oru collāl, oru viralacaippāl, oru kaṇṇimaippāl kotiyātu aṭaṅkiyirukkac ceyti viṭuvatu uṇṭu. avvāṟu aṭaṅkiyavar, eṉṉaik kaṭṭip pōṭṭu viṭṭīrkaḷ; illāviṭṭāl eṉṉa naṭakkum - naṭantirukkum - eṉpatu eṉakkē teriyātu eṉpatu uṇṭu. pāratak kataiyil tarumaṉ āṇaikkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭu niṉṟa vīmaṉ mutalāṉōr kotippaṭaiya, kaṭṭuṇṭōm, poṟuttiruppōm, kālam māṟum eṉṟu tarumaṉ kūṟiyatu karutat takkatu.
kaṭṭi muṭṭi:
	kaṭṭi 	= 	kaṭṭippaṭṭa oṉṟu, kaṭṭi.
	muṭṭi 	= 	kaṭṭi uṭaintu uṇṭāy tuṇṭu, muṭṭi.
karuppukkaṭṭi, vellakkaṭṭi, ceṅkaṟkaṭṭi, taṅkak kaṭṭi ivaṟṟil kaṭṭiyiṉ poruḷai aṟintu koḷka. iṉi maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷum, vacaiyumāy amaintatām.
ceṅkaṟkaṭṭi uṭaintataic ceṅkalmuṭṭi eṉpatu vaḻakku.  uḻavaṭaiyil kaṭṭi muṭṭitaṭṭutal oru pakutiyām. kūḻ, kaḷi kiṇṭum pōtu  kaṭṭi paṭāmalirukkak kavalaippaṭuvar. atil periyakaṭṭi ciṟiya kaṭṭiiruntāl, eṉṉa kaṭṭiyum muṭṭiyumāka irukkiṟatu eṉpatu vaḻakku. itu kaṭṭāṇi muṭṭāṇi eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭum.

kaṭṭiyam:
kaṭṭiyam:1
kaṭṭu + iyam = kaṭṭiyam.
tiṭṭamiṭṭuk kaṭṭiya collāl ōṅkik kural koṭuppatu - eccarippatu - kaṭṭiyam eṉappaṭum. kaṭṭiyaṅ kāraṉ - cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil varum kēṭaṉ.
kaṭṭiyam:2
aracaṉ tiruvōlakkattil vīṟṟirukkum pōtu, avvaracaṉ pukaḻai avaiyiluḷḷār kēṭṭu makiḻumāṟu, kaṭṭiyaṅ kūṟuvōṉ kūṟuvatu pōl amaikkap peṟum nūl kaṭṭiyam eṉappeṟum.
eṭuttuk koṇṭa pāṭal aṭiyiṉ cīr eṇṇikkaiyai aḷavākak koṇṭu periya kaṭṭiyam ciṟiya kaṭṭiyam eṉa iru vakaiyākak kūṟap peṟum. citampara aṭikaḷārāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟa tiruppōrūr murukar periya kaṭṭiyamum, tiruppōrūr murukar ciṉṉa kaṭṭiyamum ivaṟṟukkuc cāṉṟām.
ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu eḻucīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriya viruttattāṉum, piṉṉatu veṇcentuṟaiyāṉum amain tuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭṭil:
kaṭṭu + il = kaṭṭil. il = iṭam.
kuḻantaikaḷai uṟakkāṭṭat toṅkalākat tuṇiyāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu kaṭṭil eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉṉark kuḻantai paṭukka marattāl ceyyappaṭṭa nāṟkāl palakaip paṭukkaiyum kaṭṭil peyarpeṟṟatu. ataṉ piṉṉarp potumaip paṭukkaip peyarāyiṟṟu. toṭṭil kaṭṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkam karutuka.
kaṭṭu kāval:
	kaṭṭu 	= 	kōṭṭai, akaḻ, cuvar mutaliya araṇ kaṭṭākum.
	kāval	=	kāvalar, nāy ākiya kaṇkāṇippu kāval ākum.
kaṭṭumāṉam amaintatu kaṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. paḻaṅkālak kōṭṭaikaḷaik kavaṉittāl kaṭṭu kāval amaiti pulappaṭum. akaḻ, araṇ, kōṭṭai eṉpavai iruppiṉum avaṟṟaiyum takarttō kaṭantō vantu aḻivu ceyyum pakaivar iruppar. avarait taṭuttu niṟuttavum kāvalum vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu kōṭṭai vāyilil kāvalar iruppum, matilmēl kāvalar mēṭaiyumiruttalaik kaṇṭu teḷika. ataṉ eccam ikkāla vaḷamaṉai, aluvalakaṅkaḷ, karuvūlaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil iruttalai aṟika.
kaṭṭukkaṇi:
iyaṟkaiyāṉa muṭi illātavar ceyaṟkaiyāka muṭi ceytu kaṭṭutal uṇṭu. acceyaṟkai muṭiyaik kaṭṭukkaṇi eṉpatu koṅku nāṭṭu vaḻakku. kaṭṭukkaṇi = oṭṭu muṭi. kaṇi = kaṇṇi. toṭukkappaṭum kayiṟu, mālai ākiyavai kaṇṇi eṉappaṭum.
kaṭṭu cīlai:
kuḷicīlai eṉpatum tāyccīlai eṉpatum kōvaṇap poruḷaṉa. cīlai (cīrai)yil iruntu(kiḻinta cīlaiyil iruntu) kiḻittu eṭukkappaṭṭa tuṇiyaik kōvaṇamākak koṇṭatāl uṇṭākiya peyarkaḷ ivai. kōvaṇattaik kaṭṭucīlai eṉpatu viḷavaṅkōṭu vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum.
kaṭṭuttarai:
kaṭṭu + tarai = kaṭṭuttarai.
māṭu kaṭṭum toḻuvam kaṭṭuttarai eṉpatām. cuṟṟuccuvar amaintu, kaṭṭum muḷai,tīṉipōṭum kāṭi, nīr kuṭikkattakka toṭṭi āyavaṟṟaik koṇṭatu kaṭṭuttarai. kaṭṭut toḻuvam eṉpatum itu.
kaṭṭuppaṭutal:
kaṭṭuppaṭutal = kaṭṭaḷaikku uṭpaṭutal.
peṟṟavarkaḷ periyavarkaḷ eṉpatāl avarkaḷ colluvatu maṉattiṟku ovvavillai eṉṉum oruvāṟu ēṟṟuk koṇṭu naṭappatuṇṭu. ataṟkukkaṭṭuppaṭutal eṉpatu peyar. naṉṟiyaṟital kāraṇamākavum ceytārai niṉaintu kaṭṭuppaṭṭu naṭattalum uṇṭu, ūrkkūṭṭattil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭāl avarkaḷ collukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭēyāka vēṇṭum eṉṟa nilaimai aṇmaik kālam varai iruntatu. itil varum kaṭṭu āṇai eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatām. ‘C®¡f£L Ûwyhkh? eṉpatu vaḻakku moḻi. talaivaṉ coṟpaṭi niṟkum kuṭumpa āṭciyait talaikkaṭṭu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
kaṭṭuppāṭu:
kaṭṭu + pāṭu = kaṭṭuppāṭu.
kaṭṭuppaṭṭāka vēṇṭiya caṭṭa vitimuṟai; āṇai moḻi. aluvalakattil kaṭṭuppāṭē illai. vaittavar varicaitāṉ, talaimaikkuk kaṭṭuppāṭu illai! piṟaku evar kaṭṭuppāṭṭaik koḷvār? eṉpavai makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kaṭṭumaṭṭu:
	kaṭṭu 	=	varuvāykkut takkavāṟu kaṭṭuppaṭuttic celaviṭal.
	maṭṭu	=	evvaḷavutāṉ varuvāy vantālum tiṭṭappaṭutti itaṟku ivvaḷavē eṉṟu maṭṭuppaṭutti(ellai allatu aḷavu paṭutti)c celaviṭal.
kaṭṭumaṭṭu uṭaiyaṉ, kaṭṭumaṭṭāṉavaṉ eṉap potuvākak kūṟuvatum uṇṭu. iṉi itaṉaik kaṭṭu meṭṭu eṉpatum uṇṭu.
kaṭṭumāṉam:
kaṭṭumāṉam:1
kaṭṭu + māṉam = kaṭṭumāṉam. māṉam = aḷavu kaṭṭa vēṇṭiya aḷavu
kaṭṭumāṉa vēlai, kaṭṭumāṉa vilai.
kaṭṭumāṉam:2
iṭṭukkaṭṭi - puṉaintu - uraittal. uṉ kaṭṭumāṉam tāṉ ituvā? eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kaṭṭumāṉam:3
kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭutaṟku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷ.
kaṭṭumāṉam:4
vāṅkum - viṟkum - vilai. kaṭṭumāṉam ākātu; tara vāykkātu eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kaṭṭurai:
kaṭṭurai eṉpatu ōr arumaiyāṉa col; atu mikap poruṭ ceṟivuṭaiya paḻañcollākum. cilappatikārattil kaṭṭurai, uraipeṟu kaṭṭurai, kaṭṭurai kātai eṉṉum nūliṉ uṭpirivukaḷ amaintu uḷḷaṉa. kaṭṭurai eṉṉum collum mikutiyāka eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṟkup paḻaiya uraiyāciriyarkaḷ poruḷ potinta col uṟutiyuṭaiya col eṉap poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
kaṭṭumāṉam: nām oru vīṭu kaṭṭat tiṭṭamiṭṭāl mutaṟkaṇ kaṭṭumāṉap paṭam varaivōm.ataṉpiṉ kaṇṭavāṟu kaṭṭaṭam eḻuppuvōm  .atu pōlavē kaṭṭuraiyum tiṭṭamiṭṭu varaiyaṟaippaṭuttik koṇṭu eḻutappaṭa vēṇṭum. ap poḻututāṉ ataṉ amaippum poruḷum teḷivum ciṟappāka amaiyum.
kaṭṭaḻaku: cilar uṭalaip pārttatum kaṭṭāṉa uṭal eṉkiṟōm. V‹? aḷavukku viñci melivu illāmalō, ūtip pōkāmalō uriya aḷavil poruttamāka amainta nalla uṭalaiyē kaṭṭuṭal eṉṟum, attakaiyavaraiyē kaṭṭaḻakaṉ kaṭṭaḻaki eṉṟuma kūṟukiṟōm. ataṟku ēṟpavē kaṭṭuraiyum aḷavālum amaippālum kaṭṭaḻaku uṭaiyatāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.
kaṭṭu virital:moṭṭu allatu mukaiyāka irukkum pūvil maṇamum tēṉum uṇṭu; eṉiṉum, avai avvaḷavāka veḷippaṭṭup paravuvatu illai; āṉāl atu kaṭṭu virintuviṭṭāl eṅkēyō tiriyum vaṇṭum aṅkē vantu moykkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. antat tēṉilum naṟu maṇattilum taṉṉai maṟantu veḷippaṭuttik kaṟpavarai vayappaṭuttuvatē kaṭṭuraiyiṉ peṟṟikku aṭaiyāḷamākum.
kaṭṭuccōṟu: kaṭṭuc cōṟum kaṟṟa vittaiyum eṉpatu oru paḻamoḻi. kaṭṭuccōṟṟaip poti cōṟu eṉpar. poti cōṟu tuṇi mutaliyavaṟṟāl potintu vaikkappaṭṭa cōṟāka maṭṭumillāmal cuvai potintum, keṭṭup pōkāta taṉmai potintum amainta cōṟākum. atu pōlak kaṭṭuraiyum nalla naṭaiyāl potiyap peṟṟu uyarnta poruḷ potintatāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.
kaṭṭuurai: kaṭṭurai eṉṉum collai iraṇṭākap pirittāl kaṭṭu urai eṉa iraṇṭu ēvalkaḷ uṇṭākum. av ēvalai ēṟṟut tiṭṭamiṭṭuk kaṭṭi uraikkum, uraiyē kaṭṭuraiyākat tikaḻum.
iṭṭuk kaṭṭutal: kaṭṭurai eṉpatu iṭṭuk kaṭṭic collum urai eṉavum poruḷ taruvatāyiṟṟu. poyyurai, puṉainturai ākiyavaiyum kaṭṭurai eṉavē collappaṭum. eṉiṉum avaiyum kaṟpavaraiyum kēṭpavaraiyum vayappaṭuttuvaṉavākavē amaintāltāṉ nilai peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai maṟakkak kūṭātu.
kaṭṭamaippu: kaṭṭurai, ciṟappu aṭaivataṟku uvamai, paḻamoḻi, marapuccol ākiyavaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttutal vēṇṭum. nām kūṟum karuttai valiyuṟuttat takka mēṟkōḷkaḷait takka iṭattil ceyyuḷākavum iṭutal vēṇṭum. kaiyeḻuttu aḻakō kaṟpavarkkuk kāṭci iṉpam taruvatāy muttuk kōttāl pōl viḷaṅka vēṇṭum
kaṭṭu tiṭṭam: kaṭṭuraiyaip pārttavuṭaṉēyē iṉṉa poruḷ paṟṟi ikkaṭṭurai eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉpatai aṟintu koḷḷum vakaiyil muṉṉuraiyum, ikkaṭṭuraiyil amainta poruḷ muṭivu ituvē eṉpatai aṟintu koḷḷum vakaiyil muṭivuraiyum koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. iṭaiyē kūṟappaṭum ceytikaḷum ēṟattāḻa otta aḷavil pattip pirivuṭaṉ viḷaṅkutal vēṇṭum.
muṭivurai: ivaṟṟaip pōṟṟi eḻutum pulamaiyai māṇavarkaḷ vaḷarttuk koḷvatu. tērvil matippeṇ perukuvataṟku vāyppāvatuṭaṉ piṉṉāḷilum matippu mikka eḻuttāḷar āvataṟkum vakai ceyyum eṉpatu uṟuti.
kaṭṭūr:
kaṭṭūr = pācaṟai.
kaḷappōrkkuc cellum vīrar taṅkuvataṟkākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa pācaṟai vīṭukaḷ cila palavaṟṟaik koṇṭa ūr, kaṭṭūr ākum. pōr karutiyē kaṭṭappaṭṭa kuṟukiyakkālak kuṭiyiruppu ātalāl appeyar peṟṟatu. appaṭippaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ nilaitta vīṭukaḷāy amaintu pāḷaiyam eṉṟum pāḷaiyappaṭṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkalāyatum uṇṭu pāḷaiyam iṟaṅkal eṉpatu paṭaikaḷ taṅkalām.
kaṭṭai:
marattuṇṭam, kuṭṭai eṉpavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum kaṭṭai, potuvaḻakkup poruḷatu. āṉāl atu tuṟavar vaḻakkil uṭalaik kuṟittu vaḻaṅkiyatu. intak kaṭṭaikku iṉi eṉṉa vēṇṭum? intak kaṭṭai colkiṟatu eṉpar. itu potumakkaḷ vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu. uṭalvēkuvataik kaṭṭai vēkiṟatu eṉpatum atu. iṉik  kaṭṭaiyilē pōvāṉ eṉṉum vacai moḻiyil uḷḷa kaṭṭai iṭukāṭṭil kaṭṭaiyai erikka utavum viṟakuk kaṭṭaiyaik kuṟippatām.
kaṭṭaik kālaṉ:
kaṭṭai > kuṭṭai = uyarak kuṟaivu. kaṭṭaikkālaṉ eṉpatu kāl uyaram kuṟaintavaṉaik kuṟittal potu vaḻakku. neṭṭaik kālaṉukku māṟu. āṉāl, kaṭṭaikkālaṉ eṉpataṟkup paṉṟi eṉṉum poruḷ mukavai māvaṭṭa vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. itaṉaik kuṟuntāḷ ēṟṟai(perumpāṇ. 344) eṉpatu pulamakkaḷ vaḻakkākum.
kaṭṭaippakuti:
kaṭṭaippakuti = kuṟukkutteru.
neṭunteruviṉ ūṭu amainta kuṟunteru tamiḻakattil kuṟukkut teru eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. neṭiya marattaik kuṟukkē taṟippatu kaṭṭai ākum. kaṭṭai > kuṭṭai.   nīḷakkuṟaivuṭaiya teruvaik kaṭṭaip pakuti eṉpatu īḻa vaḻakku.
kaṭṭaipparutti:
kaṭṭai + parutti = kaṭṭaipparutti,
uyaramāka vaḷarāta nāṭṭup paruttiyaik kaṭṭaip parutti eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. ciṉṉa parutti eṉṟum  kūṟuvar. uyarak kuṟaivai muṉṉatu kāṭṭum, piṉṉatu, ciṉṉa iḻaikaḷai yuṭaiyatum ciṉṉa aḷavuṭaiyatum kāṭṭum.
amerikkaṉ parutti vantapiṉ peritum kaṭṭaip parutti payiriṭal aṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. ataṉaip periya parutti eṉpar.
kaṇakku:
mēṟkaṇakku, kīḻkkaṇakku eṉpavai nūl eṉṉum poruḷait tarutal aṟintatu. nūlāyntu kaṟpikka vallār  kaṇakkāyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār  eṉpatu tamiḻaṟintatu.
kaṇakku eṉpatu paṟṟiya ilakkaṇattaip paṉṉiru pāṭṭiyal pakarkiṉṟatu.
mēlkīḻk kaṇakkeṉa iruvakaik kaṇakkē	-paṉṉiru. 344
mēṟkaṇak keṉavum kīḻkkaṇak keṉavum
pāṟpaṭum vakaiyāl pakarntaṉar koḷalē	-paṉṉiru. 345
akavalum kalippā vumpari pāṭalum
patiṟṟan tāti patiṟṟaimpa tīṟā
mikuttuṭaṉ tokuppaṉa mēṟkaṇak keṉavum
veḷḷait tokaiyum avvakai eṇpeṟiṉ
eḷḷaṟu kīḻkkaṇak keṉavu koḷalē	-paṉṉiru. 346
aimpatu mutulā ainnū ṟīṟā
aivakaip pāvum poruṇeṟi marapiṟ
ṟokukkap paṭuvatu mēṟkaṇak kākum	-paṉṉiru. 347
aṭinimir pillāc ceyyuṭ ṭokuti
aṟamporuḷ iṉpam aṭukki avvat
tiṟampaṭa uraippatu kīḻkkaṇak kākum	-paṉṉiru. 348
* kīḻkkaṇakku, mēṟkaṇakku kāṇka.
kaṇakku vaḻakku:
	kaṇakku	= 	kaṇakkiṭṭut tantatu.
	vaḻakku	=	ceymuṟaikaḷāl tantatu.
eṉakkum avaṉukkum kaṇakku vaḻakku oṉṟum illai eṉa naṭappil kūṟuvatum, kaṇakku vaḻakkaip pārttuk koṭuttu viṭu eṉap pakaiyil kūṟuvatum kēṭkak cuṭiyavai.
vaḻakku = vaḻakkam, naṭaimuṟai. moy, naṉkoṭai, aṉpaḷippu eṉat tantavaiyum vaḻakkil vantuviṭum. tiṉṟataik kakku eṉpatu ciṟupiḷḷaittaṉam eṉṟāl periyavarkaḷilum attakaiyar uṇṭu eṉpataṉ cāṉṟu vaḻakku.
kaṇantuḷ:
āṭkāṭṭip paṟavai eṉpatu kaṇantuḷ paṟavaikku makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum peyar. vaḻic celvārkku vaḻippaṟippāḷar maṟaintiruppataik kāṭṭum paṟavai eṉpar.
neṭuṅkāṟ kaṇantuḷ āḷaṟi vuṟīi
āṟucel vampalar paṭaitalai peyarkkum	-kuṟun. 350
poruḷ: neṭiya kālai yuṭaiya kaṇantuṭ paṟavai āṟalai kaḷvarai aṟivuṟutti vaḻippōkiṉṟavarkaḷiṉ paṭaittiraḷai nīṅkac ceyyum.
āḷ aṟivuṟīi eṉpatu viḷaṅka arumaiyākap paṭaikkap paṭṭa peyar āṭkāṭṭip paṟavai eṉpatām. vaḻikāṭṭi, kaikāṭṭi eṉa ūrp peyarkaḷ uḷavē. itaṟkuk kaṇantuḷ eṉa ēṉ peyariṭṭaṉar?
āṟṟayal irunta iruntōṭṭu añciṟai
eṉkiṟatu kuṟuttokai (350). perutta tokutiyamainta iṟakukaḷaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya ciṟakaiyuṭaiyatu. kaṇantuḷ  tiraṇṭa kūntalaik kaṇaṅkuḻai eṉkiṟatu tirukkuṟaḷ. ataṉai eṇṇiṉāl kaṇantuḷ eṉṉum peyariṉ poruḷ viḷakkamām. periya tokutiyākiya aḻakiya ciṟaku, iruntōṭu eṉappaṭṭatu (tōṭu = tokuti). itaṉai uṭaimaiyāl kaṇantuḷ āyatu.
aṇaṅkukol āymayil kollō kaṇaṅkuḻai
mātarkol mālumeṉ neñcu	-tiruk. 1081
kaṇam:
kaṇam:1
kaṇ + am = kaṇam = kaṇṇimaip poḻutu; noṭip poḻutu.
uṉṉal kālē ūṉṟal araiyē
muṟukkal mukkāl viṭuttal oṉṟē
eḻuttiṉ oli aḷavu kaṇattāl aḷaviṭappaṭum.
kuṇameṉṉum kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟār vekuḷi
kaṇamēyum kāttal aritu	-tiruk. 29
kaṇam:2
kaṇam = kūṭṭam, tokuti.
* kaṇaṉ kāṇka.


kaṇaṉi:
kamyūṭṭar eṉṉum collaik kaṇiṉi eṉat tamiḻakam vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. īḻam ataṉaik kaṇaṉi eṉkiṉṟatu. kaṇitam, kaṇakku eṉa iruvakai vaḻakkum uḷḷamai eṇṇip pārkkalām. kaṇi, kaṇiyaṉ, kaṇippu eṉpavai kaṇi vaḻippaṭṭa coṟkaḷ.
kaṇakku, kaṇakkaṉ, kaṇakkāyar eṉpavai kaṇakku vaḻip paṭṭavai. kaṇ eṉpatu ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam eṉpatu aṟiviyal vallāṉ oruvaṉ mutukaṇṇaṉ eṉap paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattil vaḻaṅkappaṭutal koṇṭu aṟiyalām. kaṇikaṇṇaṉ, kaṇivaṉ mullai eṉṉum peyarkaḷaiyum eṇṇalām. kaṇṇakaṉ, kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum paḻaiyavaiyē.
kaṇaṉ:
kaṇ + aṉ = kaṇaṉ. kaṇam = kūṭṭam tokuti.
paṟavaiyiṉ kaṇaṅkaḷ	-nālā. 282
kaṇam > kaṇaṉ.
kaṇaṉ aṭaṅkak kaṟṟārum il	-ciṟupañ. 29
kaṇaṉ aṭaṅka = nūṟparappaṉaittum.
kaṇicam:
aḷantu koṭukkāmal kaṇṇāl aḷaviṭṭut taruvataik kaṇicam eṉpar. itaṟku ēṉ aḷantu koṇṭu; oru kaṇicamākak koṭuṅkaḷ eṉpar. piṉṉark kaiyāl ōr aḷavākat tarutal kaikkaṇicam eṉappaṭṭatu. kaṇṇāl aḷantu tarum aḷavu kaṇicam ākum. itu nellai vaḻakku.
kaṇi muṟṟūṭṭu:
kaṇiyarkku iṟaiyiliyāka (vari illāmal) vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa nilam.
paḷḷiccanta kaṇi muṟṟūṭṭu 	(ka. ka. co.a)
kaṇaiyam:
kaṇaiyam:1
kaṇ > kaṇai > kaṇaiyam.
matiṟkataviṉ uḷḷāka iṭappaṭṭa kāppumaram. tiraṇṭa valiya maram kaṇaiyam ākum. maṇveṭṭi, kōṭari āyavaṟṟukku iṭappaṭum kaṇaiyiṉai aṟiyiṉ vayiramēṟiya vaṉmaiyai uṇaralām.
x.neh.:
kaṇaiyāvatu kaippiṭiyāy amainta kōl.
	kaṇai 	= 	kaliyatu; kūriyatu (ampu) karumpiṉ kaṇai - valiyatu; muḷaiyiṭuvatu; kaṭikka iyalātatu.
kaṇaiyam:2
uṭal uḷḷuṟuppukaḷuḷ oṉṟu. kallīral maṇṇīral kaṇaiyanīr.
kaṇ:
kāṇa vāytta uṟuppu kaṇ; puṟattē kāṇum kāṭcikaḷai akattē patittu aṟivaip perukkalāl akakkaṇ, aṟivukkaṇ eṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. kāṭci eṉpataṟku aṟivup poruḷum uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇṇeṉat takum
eṉṟum,
eṇṇeṉpa ēṉai eḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum
kaṇṇeṉpa vāḻum uyirkku	-tiruk. 392
eṉṟum, eṇ eḻuttu ikaḻēleṉṟum muntaiyar moḻikaḷ kaṇṇiṉ ciṟappaik kāṭṭuvaṉavām.
eṇcāṇ uṭampukkut talaiyē talaimaiyateṉiṉum, uyariya iyakkamām mūḷai aṅkē iruppateṉiṉum, ceviyum mūkkum vāyum attalaiyilēyē iruppavai eṉiṉum kaṇṇukkuriya ciṟappup peritām.
cevik kuṟaiyiṉum kaṇkuṟaiyē, evaraiyum eḷitil parivuṟac ceyvatāka iruppatu ataṉ arumaiyālēyē ām. kaṇkaḷ iraṇṭum, nērnēr neruṅki oppavamainta amaippāl kaṇṇiṇai eṉappaṭṭaṉa. makkaḷaik kaṇmaṇikaḷ eṉṟaṉar. kaṇṇiṉ ciṟappāl kaṇṇē, kaṇṇā, kaṇṇu eṉa viḷippeyar aṉpuruvāka veḷippaṭalāyiṟṟu, iṇainta īraṭippāṭal kaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭatu.
iyaṟkaik koṭaiyāka vāyttavai ilaikaḷ. ivai nērukku nērāka īrkkil allatu taṇṭil iruntatāl kaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu:
poṉṉāṅkaṇṇi, karicilāṅkaṇṇi.
kaṇ iṇai pōlak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kayiṟṟu valai, kaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭatu. māṉ kaṇ pōl amainta kālatar - pala kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaimaiyāl - palakaṇiyām. māṉkaṇ kālatar māḷikai iṭaṅkaḷum (cilap. 5:8).
māṉkaṇ, yāṉaikkaṇ, pulikkaṇ pōlāka amainta maṭaivāy uṭaiya nīrnilai kaṇvāy ākum; aṇaikkaṭṭil iruntu pirivatu, kaṇṇāṟu. malaiyiṭai vaḻippiḷavu, irupāl malaikaḷukkum iṭaippaṭṭatākaliṉ kaṇvāy > kaṇavāy āyatu. kaipar kaṇavāy, pōlaṉ kaṇavāy, pālakkāṭṭuk kaṇavāy, kaṇavāyppaṭṭi.
kaṇṇimaikkum poḻutu kaṇam; atu oru māttirai aḷavu. kūṭṭamāy amaintatum kaṇam eṉappaṭum. e-ṭu: māṉkaṇam, pulikkaṇam.ceṟivamainta pul kaṇampul ! kaṇampullar eṉpār nāyaṉāruḷ oruvar. kaṇṇāl piṟaraik kavarpavaḷ kaṇikai
pēṇiya kaṇikaiyum piṟarnalam kāṭṭi	-maṇimē. 16:9
kaṇṇai akaḻntu appiyavar kaṇṇappar (periyapurāṇam). viṇmaṇ, nāḷ kōḷ kaṇittuk kūṟuvār, kaṇiyar, kaṇimētai, kaṇimētāvi, mutukaṇṇar eṉpār. kaṇiyam cōtiṭam kaṇikūṟal pōl taṉpolivāl kāṭṭum maram kaṇi! atu vēṅkai.
kaṇṇutal = poruntutal, karututal, kuṟittal.
kaṇṇiya varumē	-tol. 210
kapilai kaṇṇiya	-tol. 1036
kaṇpaṭuttal, kaṇpaṭai = uṟakkam.
kaṇpaṭai karutiya kaṇpaṭai nilaiyum	-tol. 1036
kaṇṇiṭattut tam tiṟam kāṭṭa vallār kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñar cilappatikāram kūṟum varippāṭaluḷ oṉṟu kaṇkūṭuvari.
kaṇpōl amainta vaṭṭavaṭi veḻuttu kaṇṇeḻuttu.
kaṇṇeḻuttup paṭutta eṇṇup palapoti	-cilap. 26:16
kaṇ > kaṇakku. kaṇakkāyar eṉa nūlaiyum nūlāy vāḷaraiyum kuṟikkum
mēṟkaṇakku; kīḻkkaṇakku; kaṇakkāyar makaṉār nakkīraṉār.
vēlaiyaiyō vēlai ceyvāraiyō kaṇkāṇippavar kaṇkāṇi. kaṅkāṇi eṉpatu koccai allatu vaḻu vaḻakku. kaṇ ēṟu paṭumeṉa vaikkappaṭum poymmai (pommai) iṉṟum viḷainilam, putumaṉaikaḷil kāṇalām.
kaṇavaraik kaṇṇāḷar eṉpatu aṉpiṉ uvakaip perukkum uvamai yamaippumām. kaṇṇiṉ maṇipōl makkaḷaik karutuvatu peṟṟōr perunilai.
kaṇṇē maṇiyē tālāṭṭu
kaṇṇamainta vaṭicaṭṭi kaṇcaṭṭi; kalam vaṉaivār ceyvatu. ataṉaic, cippilittaṭṭu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ciṟpilit taṭṭu = ciṟitu ciṟitākap piliṟṟum - vaḻiya viṭum- taṭṭu arikkaṇcaṭṭi eṉpatum atu. arikkum kaṇṇamaitta caṭṭi.
kaṇṇōṭṭam = īvu irakkam aruḷ, kaṟṟataṟku aṭaiyāḷam kaṇṇuṭaimai.
kaṇṇōṭṭam illavar kaṇṇilar	-tiruk. 577
kaṇṇuk kaṇikalam kaṇṇōṭṭam	-tiruk. 575
kaṇṇuṭaiyar eṉpavar kaṟṟōr	-tiruk. 393
kaṇṇōṭal = orupakkam cārtal.
muṟai eṉappaṭuvatu, kaṇṇōṭātu uyirvauval	-kalit.133
paṟaivakaikaḷuḷ orupakkam aṭippatu, irupakkam aṭippatu eṉa iruvakai uṇṭu. avai orukaṭpaṟai, irukaṭpaṟai eṉpaṉa. kākattiṟkuk kaṇ iraṇṭēṉum kaṇmaṇi oṉṟē. oru  kaṇṇukku  orukāṭci.
kaṇpōl matittal kaṇṇiyam. kaṇṇāl aḷaviṭutal matippiṭutal kaṇittal kaṇakkiṭṭut tulliyamāka evaṟṟaiyum kāṭṭum karuvi kaṇiṉi.
kaṇṇaḻaku mikkār kaṇṇaṉaiyar. kaṇṇaṉ vaḻip peyarum kaṇṇi vaḻippeyarum perukiṉa kaṇṇaṉ, mutukaṇṇaṉ, kaṇikaṇṇaṉ iṉṉavai āṇpāṟ peyarkaḷāyiṉ kaṇṇammai, kaṇṇāyi, aṅkayaṟkaṇṇi, kuvaḷaik kaṇṇi, tāmaraikkaṇṇi - iṉṉavai peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ āyiṉa.
kaṇṇukku maiyiṭuvār uṇṭu, aṇikalam iṭuvār illai! mūkku kātu, kaḻuttu, kai, kāl, iṭuppu āyavaṟṟil eṇṇaṟṟa aṇikaḷai aṇivāraip paṇṭumutal iṉṟuvarai kaṇṭu varukiṉṟōm. āṉāl kaṇṇukku aṇi uṇṭā? ām uṇṭu!
maṟṟai uṟuppukaḷiṉ aṇikalaṅkaḷil aḻaku enta aḷavu uṇṭō, anta aḷavu avalamum uṇṭu! āṉāl, avalamē illāta aṇi oṉṟaṉṟu palavaṟṟaik koṇṭatu kaṇ! ataṉaik kāṭṭiya perumai vaḷḷuvarkku uṇṭu!
kaṇṇukku aṇikalam kaṇṇōṭṭam
eṉṟatu (575) maṭṭumā? aḵtiṉṟēl puṇ eṉkiṟārē vaḷḷuvar.
kaṇṇōṭṭamē pēraḻaku; kaṇṇōṭṭattiltāṉ ulaka iyakkamē uḷḷatu eṉṟellām viyantu viyantu uraikkiṟārē! puṉkaṇīr aṉpuccāṉṟu eṉṟārē avar. !
civaṉiya ulakam muḻu mutalvaṉai mukkaṇṇaṉākak kaṇṭatu! avaṉ kaṇṇai, neṟṟik kaṇ eṉṟatu.
peṇmaiyaip paḻikka allatu nilaiyāmaiyaic cuṭṭa vantārum vēṟkaṇṇaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇi mayaṅkātē! ivaḷ kōṟkaṇṇaḷ āvāḷ eṉpatai maṟavātē eṉṟaṉar! āṉāl, caṅkac cāṉṟōr kaṇṇakaṉār, kāviyak kaṇṇakiyār, tirut toṇṭar kaṇṇappar, meyyiyal kaṇṇuṭaiya vaḷḷalār, pāratiyāriṉ kaṇṇaṉ, kaṇṇammāḷ, tiraip paṭappukaḻ kaṇṇatācaṉār eṉa ettuṇaip pērkaḷ cāvā vāḻviṉarāy - neñcil vāḻvārāy- irukkiṉṟaṉarē. ivarkaḷ, nilai yāmaiyai veṉṟa nilaipēṟṟāḷar eṉpataṉ cāṉṟāḷarkaḷ allarō!
kaṇ eṉpataṉait toṭakkamākak koṇṭa, vaḻaṅku coṟkaḷtām ettaṉai! ettaṉai!
‘kaṇ’ eṉpataṉai īṟākak koṇṭa, vaḻaṅku coṟkaḷ tām ettaṉai !ettaṉai!

kaṇvaittal
kuṟippu:
	1.	kaṇ eṉpatu ataṉ valliṉat tiripāki kaṭ eṉa iṇaiyum iṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. kaṇṇiṉ muṉ ka, ca, ta, pa ākiya valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻiyāka avai irukkum. kaṇ cevi > kaṭcevi.
	2.  kaṇṭapaṭi, kaṇṭa maṭṭum, kaṇṭavāṟu, kaṇṭatum kaḻiyatum, kaṇṭavar viṇṭilar eṉpavai makkaḷ vaḻakkil peruka vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
kaṇ īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ cila.
